## 🔧 模块定义

### 1. Eth

#### 基本信息

- **UUID**: `f6164118-a134-467d-85f8-8166f3a92a14`
- **模块名称**: `Eth`
- **类别**: `VENDOR_SPECIFIC_MODULE_DEFINITION`
- **多重性**: [0..infinite]
- **支持的变体**:
  - VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - VARIANT-POST-BUILD

#### 模块描述

Ethernet Driver Infineon AURIX TriCore Tc3xx Ethernet Controller. DrvEth_Tc3xxEthAsr provides hardware independent interface for initialization, configuration, and data transmission of the Ethernet Controller. It offers interfaces to the Ethernet Interface, Ethernet Transceiver Driver, and Ethernet Switch Driver.

#Ethernet #Driver #Communication
#AUTOSAR

######## 3. EthZeroCopyBufferStartOffset

- **UUID**: `032ba714-b320-4d2b-8d1b-46e72b699481`
- **参数名**: `EthZeroCopyBufferStartOffset`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..255]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Configure the buffer start offset for zero copy extension.

This offset will be added to each buffer start. The additional space can be used for transmission on other bus systems. It will not be used for external provided buffers.

Note: The size of the offset must be a multiple of the expected controller alignment size. Value 0 is allowed to disable the offset.

####### BOOLEAN 类型参数

######## 1. EthDevErrorDetect

- **UUID**: `ab807966-ccab-4a51-bf7e-d780d752bf84`
- **参数名**: `EthDevErrorDetect`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `true`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter switches the Development Error Detection and Notification ON or OFF.
true:	Development error detection is enabled.
false:	Development error detection is disabled.

Note: In general, the development error detection is recommended during pre-test phase. It is not recommended to enable the development error detection in production code due to increased runtime and ROM needs.

######## 5. EthEnableRxInterrupt

- **UUID**: `d96fb040-80dc-4071-ba9d-077a245fc05c`
- **参数名**: `EthEnableRxInterrupt`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `true`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false

######## 6. EthEnableTxInterrupt

- **UUID**: `af31c89f-4e05-4025-a955-7ece4984eeb8`
- **参数名**: `EthEnableTxInterrupt`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `true`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines if the transmit interrupt is used.

Restrictions: 'EthEnableTxInterrupt' and 'EthIfEnableTxInterrupt' must be set to the same value.

######## 7. EthEnableExplicitBufferMapping

- **UUID**: `cc6bd57f-03d3-474a-8e4b-5c7e6b1138ef`
- **参数名**: `EthEnableExplicitBufferMapping`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `true`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter is deprecated: This feature is now always on. The configured value has no effect. There are five memory sections that can be used to define the memory locations of the driver's buffers and descriptors:
- RX_BUFFER for the reception buffers
- TX_BUFFER for the transmission buffers
- RX_DESCRIPTOR for the reception descriptors
- TX_DESCRIPTOR for the transmission descriptors
- DESCRIPTOR_BASE_ADDRESS_TABLE for the descriptor base address table.
These memory sections are used for the buffers and descriptors of all EthCtrlConfigs (Ethernet channels) of this driver.

The final mapping is done via AUTOSAR Memory Map, whereas the user must implement the buffer specific memory sections within the memory map by himself according to the hardware requirements.

######## 8. EthRuntimeMeasurementSupport

- **UUID**: `a4ca82e2-7435-4b37-b5c8-a09d61248157`
- **参数名**: `EthRuntimeMeasurementSupport`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: If enabled runtime measurement points are added to the BSW module code and are added to the module configuration of MICROSAR RTM. The available measurement points can be seen and configured in the RTM module configuration. Availability of the MICROSAR RTM module is a prerequisite to make use of the runtime measurement functionality.
Runtime measurement should be disabled in production code as measurement points may inflict additional runtime.

######## 9. EthEnableZeroCopyExtensions

- **UUID**: `cba2bf48-39d0-4297-872d-ed563bd10a1a`
- **参数名**: `EthEnableZeroCopyExtensions`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines if the Zero-Copy Extensions for reception buffers and transmission buffers are enabled.

Impact on handling of:

Reception Buffers:
The Rx Buffers aren't implicitly released after the RxIndication() to the upper layer by the driver itself anymore but must be explicitly released by the Upper Layer. This mechanism allows to keep buffers for explicit usage until the EthIf_ReleaseRxBuffer() API is called.

Transmission Buffers:
In addition to using internal Tx Buffers provided by the driver during ProvideTxBuffer() a User is able to inject external buffers used for transmission with the call to ProvideExtTxBuffer().

######## 10. EthEnableHeaderAccessApi

- **UUID**: `1914b6de-b1c0-47eb-b2c7-3708a5c3f1fd`
- **参数名**: `EthEnableHeaderAccessApi`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines if APIs are provided to allow access to the Ethernet frame header.

Usually the Ethernet frame header is cut off during reception or is assembled during transmission by the Ethernet driver. To read the header information during reception or to overwrite the header that is assembled by the driver druing transmission these APIs can be used.

######## 11. EthUsePeripheralAccessApi

- **UUID**: `3531516b-5dbe-4a21-a5f0-740375442025`
- **参数名**: `EthUsePeripheralAccessApi`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines if the Peripheral Access APIs are used for accessing protected registers related to the Ethernet driver.

Depending on the derivative some Ethernet driver related register may be only accessibly in a Privileged Mode. If the Ethernet driver is not running in a necessary Privileged Mode then the OS has to support the register access by Peripheral Access APIs.

######## 12. EthSafeBswChecks

- **UUID**: `c2ad7677-ae53-4ed7-861e-33e926dabe8e`
- **参数名**: `EthSafeBswChecks`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Selects if safety checks are used. These checks improve robustness as e.g. invalid parameters are checked before function is executed. This attribute has to be enabled for safety projects where this component is mapped to an ASIL partition.

######## 13. EthGetEtherStatsApi

- **UUID**: `414c362c-f070-43c1-aa4c-45af653a387b`
- **参数名**: `EthGetEtherStatsApi`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This paramter enables or disables the optional GetRxStats / GetTxStats APIs.

######## 14. EthGetAndResetMeasurementDataApi

- **UUID**: `cfe7f72b-90e1-4ffd-b1d6-594d0728d895`
- **参数名**: `EthGetAndResetMeasurementDataApi`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `true`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter enables/disables the function Eth_GetAndResetMeasurementData() which is used to get or reset the vendor specific frame drop statistics due to insufficient buffers.

######## 15. EthEnableRawTxApi

- **UUID**: `a602dffd-4112-4ffd-a3f3-ad1f2cb727a9`
- **参数名**: `EthEnableRawTxApi`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This boolean value enables/disables the availabillity for the "raw TX" API. This API provides the possibility to transmit a frame without performing any write operation on the given buffer. Thus, the given buffer must be formatted correctly for an Ethernet frame! The API will check for which queue to transmit, if QoS is used.
NOTE: If you want to use TxConfirmation via "raw TX" API, then you have to provide a special callout: EthConfigSet/EthCtrlConfig/EthRawTxConfirmationCallout

####### ENUMERATION 类型参数

######## 1. EthOsIsrCategory

- **UUID**: `3f8a019e-b2b9-45ae-b87a-01bfe7ef78ec`
- **参数名**: `EthOsIsrCategory`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `CATEGORY_2`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `CATEGORY_1` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `CATEGORY_2` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `NO_OS_SUPPORT` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **描述**: This parameter specifies how the interrupt routines will be handled.

If CATEGORY_1 or CATEGORY_2 is selected the Os will handle the interrupts according to the seleted category.
For interrupt handling without support from Os the value NO_OS_SUPPORT must be selected.

######## 2. EthRuntimeEnvironment

- **UUID**: `31e1fffa-f343-4030-b637-9333e2c13432`
- **参数名**: `EthRuntimeEnvironment`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `ETH_AUTOSAR_OS`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `ETH_AUTOSAR_OS` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `ETH_GREENHILLS_INTEGRITY` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **描述**: This parameter defines the runtime environment the Ethernet driver is executed on.

ETH_AUTOSAR_OS:
The driver is executed in a common AUTOSAR OS environment.

ETH_GREENHILLS_INTEGRITY:
The driver is executed in a Greenhills INTEGRITY OS environment.

####### FUNCTION-NAME 类型参数

######## 1. EthPreCtrlInitUserCallout

- **UUID**: `ed000062-4088-48e9-9b3b-d425f8152961`
- **参数名**: `EthPreCtrlInitUserCallout`
- **数据类型**: FUNCTION-NAME
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **函数变体**:
  - 默认值: `NULL_PTR`
- **描述**: This parameter defines the function to be called before the driver initializes a Ethernet controller.

The function that is provided here has to have following signature:
void <User_PreCtrlInitCallout>(uint8 EthCtrlIdx);


NULL_PTR as value disables the functionality.

With the passed EthCtrlIdx, representing the Symbolic Name Value of the respective Ethernet Controller, one can distinguish between the initialization context that is currently executed.

######## 2. EthPostCtrlInitUserCallout

- **UUID**: `b1ea3eba-5f60-4ae3-ae7d-83582ddf92eb`
- **参数名**: `EthPostCtrlInitUserCallout`
- **数据类型**: FUNCTION-NAME
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **函数变体**:
  - 默认值: `NULL_PTR`
- **描述**: This parameter defines the function to be called after the driver has initialized a Ethernet controller.

The function that is provided here has to have following signature:
void <User_PostCtrlInitCallout>(uint8 EthCtrlIdx);


NULL_PTR as value disables the functionality.

With the passed EthCtrlIdx, representing the Symbolic Name Value of the respective Ethernet Controller, one can distinguish between the initialization context that is currently executed.

######## 3. EthRxBufWarnLvlCallout

- **UUID**: `393ff977-69d8-4de8-bdf2-df7f27bde452`
- **参数名**: `EthRxBufWarnLvlCallout`
- **数据类型**: FUNCTION-NAME
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **函数变体**:
  - 默认值: `NULL_PTR`
- **描述**: This parameter defines the function to be called when the driver detects that all the configured reception buffers within a EthRxBufConfig are in use by the software. This condition occurs, if the DMAC does not have a free buffer to copy additional incoming frame data to, which means that frame loss can occur if the situation persists.

The function that is provided here has to have following signature:
void User_RxBufWarnLvlCallout(uint8 CtrlIdx, uint8 BufRingIdx);
Where CtrlIdx is the unique identifier for the affected Ethernet controller and BufRingIdx the controller local reception buffer ring (BufRingIdx equal to 0 to amount of buffer rings configured minus one).

NULL_PTR as value disables the functionality.

########## 3. EthCtrlEnableTxInterrupt

Restrictions: 'EthCtrlEnableTxInterrupt' must be equal for all controllers and equal to 'EthEnableTxInterrupt'

########## 4. EthCtrlEnableMacWriteAccess

- **UUID**: `9f652936-bfd5-483e-9370-c66cd3008ce9`
- **参数名**: `EthCtrlEnableMacWriteAccess`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines if access to an NvM block containing the MAC address and the corresponding API is enabled.

########## 5. EthCtrlEnableQoS

- **UUID**: `9f30d178-2514-4049-8614-46d26a7a5996`
- **参数名**: `EthCtrlEnableQoS`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines if QoS (Quality of Service) is enabled.

VLAN tagged frames are prioritized according to their VLAN priority. The priority handling is taken into account during reception and transmission of frames.

########## 6. EthCtrlEnablePtp

- **UUID**: `15e67c24-dae9-4452-91b4-e6ad3c47ffd8`
- **参数名**: `EthCtrlEnablePtp`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines if Precision-Time-Protocol support is enabled or not. PTP is mostly used in combination with Audio Video Bridging (AVB)

########## 7. EthEnableAVBConformance

- **UUID**: `e2ae304a-7839-4bee-9471-2bb9c2e3d622`
- **参数名**: `EthEnableAVBConformance`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines if the QoS/FQTSS features a used for a AVB or generalized usecase.
In the AVB usecase a maximum  of 75% of the Port Transmit Rate can be used for traffic class A and B. Only frames with a frametype related to AVB are passed to the queues for traffic class A and B.

########## 8. EthCtrlEnableVlanIDBasedFramesQueuing

- **UUID**: `b1f4f946-6d5d-4808-9a15-b8454e750859`
- **参数名**: `EthCtrlEnableVlanIDBasedFramesQueuing`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines if the Ethernet frames are queued based on VLAN ID
VLAN tagged frames are assigned to the TX/RX queues according to their VLAN ID. The VLAN-ID and the queue mapping shall be configured by the user.

########## 9. EthCtrlEnableQueueManagement

- **UUID**: `33973e7a-bdc0-4450-8460-b4d1fae69fce`
- **参数名**: `EthCtrlEnableQueueManagement`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false

########## 10. EthCtrlEnableOsfMode

- **UUID**: `d2625ab7-0979-4032-a9f3-2b1eba41316f`
- **参数名**: `EthCtrlEnableOsfMode`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `true`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines whether OSF mode is used for frame transmission. When OSF mode is enabled, the processing of a second Tx-descriptor is started before the first one is transmitted completely. This results in increased throughput in transmission.

########## 11. EthCtrlEnablePpsEventOnTimeApi

- **UUID**: `a16e49c7-872d-4953-b33a-16f1bece9026`
- **参数名**: `EthCtrlEnablePpsEventOnTimeApi`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter enables the ArmPpsEventOnTime Api. The Api is used for PPS sub-module of the Ethernet MAC to trigger an event on the PPS line at the given globalTime.

######### STRING 类型参数

########## 1. EthCtrlPhysAddr

- **UUID**: `424ff68f-f4b1-4015-a92a-46689984c1dd`
- **参数名**: `EthCtrlPhysAddr`

- **需要索引**: false
- **字符串变体**:

- **描述**: This parameter defines the Controller Physical Hardware Address (MAC-Address). The input format has to match xx:xx:xx:xx:xx:xx, where x stands for a hex value between 0 and F.

######### ENUMERATION 类型参数

########## 1. EthCtrlEnableTrafficShaping

- **UUID**: `05caa505-e596-4ffa-8383-9deb0c413813`
- **参数名**: `EthCtrlEnableTrafficShaping`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `NO_TRAFFIC_SHAPING`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `NO_TRAFFIC_SHAPING` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `STATIC_TRAFFIC_SHAPING` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DYNAMIC_TRAFFIC_SHAPING` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **描述**: This parameter defines whether Traffic Shaping is used. Therefore the ethernet controller uses an algorithm like CBS (Credit Based Shaping) for limiting the maximum bandwidth per queue.

########## 2. EthCtrlMiiInterface

- **UUID**: `5d6ed626-12fe-4a40-b907-e1ffc80428a3`

  - `ETH_RMII_MODE` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `ETH_RGMII_MODE` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **描述**: This parameter defines the interface type used to connect PHY and MAC.

Please refere to the derivative's reference manual and the board schematic to retrieve the correct management interface type.

########## 3. EthCtrlCsrClkRange

- **UUID**: `094773ef-458f-4929-b4d6-78f570a68393`
- **参数名**: `EthCtrlCsrClkRange`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `CLK_20_35_MHZ`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `CLK_20_35_MHZ` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `CLK_35_60_MHZ` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `CLK_60_100_MHZ` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `CLK_100_150_MHZ` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `CLK_150_250_MHZ` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `CLK_250_300_MHZ` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `CLK_300_500_MHZ` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `CLK_500_800_MHZ` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **描述**: The CSR clock range determines the frequency of the MDC clock according to CSR clock frequnecy.

The MDC clock should be approximately between 1.0 MHz to 2.5 MHz.

########## 4. EthCtrlPrtSpdSel

- **UUID**: `1538963a-34e8-4d9e-8f50-8d24d460811c`

######### FUNCTION-NAME 类型参数

########## 1. EthCtrlPeripheralRegionOsIdentifier

- **UUID**: `d705db2e-33ff-49bd-ae62-c731f11aca19`
- **参数名**: `EthCtrlPeripheralRegionOsIdentifier`
- **数据类型**: FUNCTION-NAME
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Unique C Identifier which is used by the Ethernet Controller driver for accessing the OS configured peripheral region.

########## 2. EthRxIndicationCallout

- **UUID**: `5247fdab-0071-4d60-8f80-7e3fafb93a32`
- **参数名**: `EthRxIndicationCallout`
- **数据类型**: FUNCTION-NAME
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **函数变体**:
  - 默认值: `NULL_PTR`
- **描述**: This parameter defines the alternative function to be called upon the reception of an Ethernet frame instead of the EthIf_RxIndication.

NULL_PTR as value disables the functionality and the EthIf_RxIndication is called again.

The signature of the function specified in this parameter has to match the signature of the EthIf_RxIndication used in the delivery.

########## 3. EthTxConfirmationCallout

- **UUID**: `90e35b55-8320-4c78-8617-6bfcf2c5a4a2`
- **参数名**: `EthTxConfirmationCallout`
- **数据类型**: FUNCTION-NAME
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **函数变体**:
  - 默认值: `NULL_PTR`
- **描述**: This parameter defines the alternative function to be called upon the confirmation of an Ethernet frame instead of the EthIf_TxConfirmation.

NULL_PTR as value disables the functionality and the EthIf_TxConfirmation is called again.

The signature of the function specified in this parameter has to match the signature of the EthIf_TxConfirmation used in the delivery.

########## 4. EthRawTxConfirmationCallout

- **UUID**: `0425e5b0-c7f5-426b-ad66-365d5cac3220`
- **参数名**: `EthRawTxConfirmationCallout`
- **数据类型**: FUNCTION-NAME
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **函数变体**:
  - 默认值: `NULL_PTR`
- **描述**: This parameter defines the used Tx confirmation callout for EthEnableRawTxApi.
WARNING: EthIf_TxConfirmation() is never called for raw transmission requests!
The signature of the function specified in this parameter has to match the signature of the EthIf_TxConfirmation used in the delivery.

######### 1. EthRxBufConfig

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `d22e3856-672b-49ca-a5e2-a15464127dd5`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `EthRxBufConfig`
- **多重性**: [1..infinite]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

This container includes the configuration of an Rx buffer.

########## 参数配置

此容器包含 **4** 个参数：

########### INTEGER 类型参数

############ 1. EthRxSegmentNumber

- **UUID**: `f1b87058-cd85-4537-a817-8463907870c4`
- **参数名**: `EthRxSegmentNumber`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `2`
- **取值范围**: [2..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value defines the number of Rx segments the receiver buffer is assembled of.

############ 2. EthRxExtSegmentNumber

- **UUID**: `2185694c-4c71-49ad-a1c9-f1692eeea1b4`
- **参数名**: `EthRxExtSegmentNumber`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `2`
- **取值范围**: [1..255]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value specifies the maximum expected number of extended buffers that be used for frame receiving.
The amount of normal buffers (Parameter 'Number of Rx Segments') ist used to generate the amount of RX descriptors and normal RX buffers. Due to the delayed RX buffer release for the Zero Copy feature RX descriptors could be blocked and the paket reception would stop. During a delayed buffer release the extended buffers are used to assign them to the RX descriptor, while a buffer release is still pending.
The packet number that can be received during a burst may be limited to 'Number of Rx Segments', but with the additional buffers it is still possible receive this burst while buffer releases are still pending.

Restrictions: The amount of normal and additional buffers must not exceed a total of 255.

############ 3. EthRxSegmentSize

- **UUID**: `bc47ae79-1fff-4478-9a81-e62e4558b25a`
- **参数名**: `EthRxSegmentSize`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `1522`
- **取值范围**: [60..1600]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines the size of a segment the receive buffer is built of.

Multiple of these segments are used to hold a complete Ethernet frame.

############ 4. EthRxMaxFrameSize

- **UUID**: `11370d7d-a2f8-4ddd-a941-5741e7604b6f`
- **参数名**: `EthRxMaxFrameSize`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `1522`
- **取值范围**: [60..1600]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines the maximum length of an Ethernet frame, which is allowed to be received.

######### 2. EthTxBufConfig

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `09f5a388-3a22-4095-84fd-0bfa8d8ce621`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `EthTxBufConfig`
- **多重性**: [1..infinite]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

This container includes the configuration of a Tx buffer.

########## 参数配置

此容器包含 **2** 个参数：

########### INTEGER 类型参数

############ 1. EthCtrlTxBufLenByte

- **UUID**: `93403e0d-d8bb-4016-83e6-96edb188a005`
- **参数名**: `EthCtrlTxBufLenByte`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `1522`
- **取值范围**: [64..1522]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value limits the maximum length (including Ethernet header + payload + CRC) of the Ethernet frame in bytes.
In case VLAN is enabled the Ethernet header length is 18, otherwise 14. The maximum payload length is 1500 bytes and the minimum length is 46 bytes. The CRC consists of 4 bytes.

Example:
- Configuring a buffer that shall hold a VLAN tagged Ethernet frame with a maximum payload of 1500 bytes, the overall frame length is 1500 bytes payload + 18 bytes header + 4 bytes CRC = 1522 bytes.
- Configuring a buffer that shall hold an Ethernet frame without VLAN tag and a minimum payload of 46 bytes, the overall frame length is 46 bytes payload + 14 bytes header + 4 bytes CRC = 64 bytes.

############ 2. EthTxBufTotal

- **UUID**: `36542667-1448-49fb-9b51-74689cd65603`
- **参数名**: `EthTxBufTotal`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `2`
- **取值范围**: [2..255]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value specifies the number of transmit buffers.

############ 2. EthDeviceIdentifier

- **参数名**: `EthDeviceIdentifier`

  - 最小长度: 1

############ 3. EthResManagerPassword

- **UUID**: `d6ed4419-ff5a-4b34-bc02-348bbbaae518`
- **参数名**: `EthResManagerPassword`
- **数据类型**: LINKER-SYMBOL
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **字符串变体**:
  - 默认值: `NULL`
  - 最大长度: 254
  - 最小长度: 1
- **描述**: This paramter defines the password used to grant access to the Resource Manager of INTEGRITY.

This password is used within calls to the INTEGRITY Resource Manager (e.g. RequestResource()) to retrieve the needed OS objects for IO Device, register memory region and so on.

If no password is used by the Resource Manager NULL must be provided as value to signal the omit of the password check.

############ 4. EthRegMemRegionPool

- **字符串变体**:
  - 默认值: `__ghs_VirtualMemoryRegionPool`
  - 最小长度: 1
- **描述**: This parameter defines the Memory Region Pool where the virtual Memory Region to map the registers into the virtual address space is drawn from.

- **数据类型**: FUNCTION-NAME
- **多重性**: [1..1]

- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **函数变体**:
  - 默认值: `NULL_PTR`
- **描述**: This parameter defines a user function that shall be called at the start of the ISR.

The function declaration must be provided by a header file added to EthAdditionalIncludes in the EthGeneral configuration container.

NULL_PTR: No user function shall be called.

The function declaration must be provided by a header file added to EthAdditionalIncludes in the EthGeneral configuration container.

Function signature shall be as followed:

NULL_PTR: No user function shall be called.

######### 17. EthZeroCopyConfig

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `554c3bed-768d-4ac3-933f-7cbbe3cefa27`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `EthZeroCopyConfig`
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

This container contains the configuration for Zero-Copy related parameters.

########## 参数配置

此容器包含 **3** 个参数：

########### INTEGER 类型参数

############ 1. EthCtrlMaxExtBufNum

- **UUID**: `14f72291-2e68-4265-a299-e48697a75c78`
- **参数名**: `EthCtrlMaxExtBufNum`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `2`
- **取值范围**: [1..255]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value specifies the maximum number of external transmission buffers that can be injected simultaniously. These buffers are provided by the user and the driver only keeps track of their state when used.

########### FUNCTION-NAME 类型参数

############ 1. EthCtrlExtTxBufConfirmationCallout

- **UUID**: `c4a90548-e8d0-4a35-8959-aa2f9177df7a`
- **参数名**: `EthCtrlExtTxBufConfirmationCallout`
- **数据类型**: FUNCTION-NAME
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **函数变体**:
  - 默认值: `NULL_PTR`
- **描述**: This parameter defines the alternative function to be called upon the confirmation of an Ethernet frame based on external transmission buffers.
If the EthIf is not present in the SIP, then this parameter is mandatory, else optional.

Means: If the EthIf is present in the SIP and this callout is given, then all external buffer TX confirmations are calling the EthCtrlExtTxBufConfirmationCallout.

The signature of the function specified in this parameter has to match the signature of the EthIf_TxConfirmation used in the delivery.

############ 2. EthAddRxSegmentsDepletedCallout

- **UUID**: `54a3c642-d170-4f37-902d-f23901eb100f`
- **参数名**: `EthAddRxSegmentsDepletedCallout`
- **数据类型**: FUNCTION-NAME
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **函数变体**:
  - 默认值: `NULL_PTR`
- **描述**: This parameter defines a function, which will be called, if all additional Zero Copy Rx Buffers of a queue are depleted and received frames are being discarded, because the user application does not release the Rx Buffers.

The callout is declared as pointer to function
void AddRxSegmentsDepletedCallout(uint8 ctrlIdx, uint8 rxQueueId)

######### 18. EthQueueManagement

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `c88589c4-31a4-4686-ab75-1e13f0030eee`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `EthQueueManagement`
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

This container includes the configuration rx queue configuration. Rx queues in this container are used to map different receivers. (other than standard Ethif Module)
This container includes the configuration which is necessary for extended Rx queue managment.
Based on the rx queue filtering, rx indication of the respective queue is provided to a external module.

########## 引用关系

此容器包含 **1** 个引用：

########### REFERENCE 类型引用

############ 1. EthQueue0RxBufConfigRef

- **UUID**: `94d9245b-b40a-4be4-b8e0-89749318cc67`
- **引用名**: `EthQueue0RxBufConfigRef`
- **引用类型**: REFERENCE
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **目标**: `EthRxBufConfig`
- **完整路径**: `/MICROSAR/Eth_30_Tc3xx/Eth/EthConfigSet/EthCtrlConfig/EthRxBufConfig`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Reference to the default queue, where all received frames, which do not match configured reception filter of any other queue, will be placed.

########## 配置容器

本模块包含 **1** 个配置容器：

########### 1. EthExtendedRxQueue

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `886271e8-4315-414c-9afc-9daa50c80cbe`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `EthExtendedRxQueue`
- **多重性**: [1..infinite]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

This container includes the configuration for the Reception Queue Management. If several of the matching parameters in this container are configured, all criteria must be satisfied for the frame to be mapped to the respective queue.

############ 参数配置

此容器包含 **2** 个参数：

############# INTEGER 类型参数

############## 1. EthQueueIndex

- **UUID**: `648e4dab-3eb7-4048-bdfa-80f4979dde2b`
- **参数名**: `EthQueueIndex`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..255]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines the index of the queue. This is set by DaVinci Configurator automatically.

############# FUNCTION-NAME 类型参数

############## 1. EthExtendedRxIndicationCallout

- **UUID**: `f55f52a4-2290-4948-97a6-0ff59b22dd94`
- **参数名**: `EthExtendedRxIndicationCallout`
- **数据类型**: FUNCTION-NAME
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **函数变体**:
  - 默认值: `NULL_PTR`
- **描述**: This parameter defines the queue specific alternative function to be called upon the reception of an Ethernet frame instead of the EthIf_RxIndication.
Deleting this parameter disables the functionality and the EthIf_RxIndication is called again.

The callout is declared as pointer to function
void ExtendedRxIndicationCallout(uint8 ctrlIdx, uint8 rxBufIdx, Eth_FrameType frameType, uint8* rxBufStartPtr, uint16 offsetToHeaderStart, uint16 offsetToPayloadStart, uint16 payloadLength)

/MICROSAR/Eth/EthGeneral/EthAdditionalIncludes parameter is used to add the header file for this callout.

此容器包含 **1** 个引用：

- **作用域**: LOCAL

- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Reference to the EthRxBufferConfig which is used by this queue.

########## 参数配置

此容器包含 **7** 个参数：

########### INTEGER 类型参数

############ 1. EthCtrlMemoryRemappingOffset

- **UUID**: `32a781a7-4e49-4dad-8f9a-82d660dbd630`
- **参数名**: `EthCtrlMemoryRemappingOffset`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: The offset between local memory address (CPU address) and global memory address (SoC address). This parameter needs to be set when the memory remapping is enabled.

############ 2. EthCtrlLocalAddressLowerBorder

- **UUID**: `7257380a-f382-4ff1-8700-eb64997c3746`
- **参数名**: `EthCtrlLocalAddressLowerBorder`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Lower border for local memory address (CPU address). This parameter needs to be set when the memory remapping is enabled.

############ 3. EthCtrlLocalAddressUpperBorder

- **UUID**: `2af9f3ca-7d67-44eb-9a3b-b11e7565053e`
- **参数名**: `EthCtrlLocalAddressUpperBorder`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Upper border for local memory address (CPU address). This parameter needs to be set when the memory remapping is enabled. This address is the first address value excluded from the local memory address range.

############ 4. EthCtrlGlobalAddressLowerBorder

- **UUID**: `7bcd05c6-0e61-4ad8-9bac-66557f86e34f`
- **参数名**: `EthCtrlGlobalAddressLowerBorder`

- **多重性**: [1..1]

- **描述**: Lower border for global memory address (SoC address). This parameter needs to be set when the memory remapping is enabled.

############ 5. EthCtrlGlobalAddressUpperBorder

- **UUID**: `9d99033f-8552-47d6-8e23-ae07ce1d558c`
- **参数名**: `EthCtrlGlobalAddressUpperBorder`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]

- **取值范围**: [0..]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false

- **描述**: Upper border for global memory address (SoC address). This parameter needs to be set when the memory remapping is enabled. This address is the first address value excluded from the global memory address range.

########### BOOLEAN 类型参数

############ 1. EthCtrlEnableMemoryRemapping

- **UUID**: `24efd349-619a-445f-999d-c12b00e1883b`
- **参数名**: `EthCtrlEnableMemoryRemapping`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines if the memory remapping is enabled. This parameter should be enabled when the memory region (which is used for Ethernet descriptors/buffers) have different CPU address (the address in the view of CPU) and SoC address (the address in the view of the peripheries, e. g. the DMA).

############ 2. EthCtrlMemoryRemappingNegativeOffset

- **UUID**: `708fbfe6-5120-4e59-9330-2fdc6398f9f2`
- **参数名**: `EthCtrlMemoryRemappingNegativeOffset`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines if the memory remapping offset is negative. Therefore, the local address borders shall have a greater value than the global address borders.

####### 2. EthRxBufConfig

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `d22e3856-672b-49ca-a5e2-a15464127dd5`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `EthRxBufConfig`
- **多重性**: [1..infinite]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

This container includes the configuration of an Rx buffer.

######## 参数配置

此容器包含 **4** 个参数：

######### INTEGER 类型参数

########## 1. EthRxSegmentNumber

- **UUID**: `f1b87058-cd85-4537-a817-8463907870c4`
- **参数名**: `EthRxSegmentNumber`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `2`
- **取值范围**: [2..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value defines the number of Rx segments the receiver buffer is assembled of.

########## 2. EthRxExtSegmentNumber

- **UUID**: `2185694c-4c71-49ad-a1c9-f1692eeea1b4`
- **参数名**: `EthRxExtSegmentNumber`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `2`
- **取值范围**: [1..255]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value specifies the maximum expected number of extended buffers that be used for frame receiving.
The amount of normal buffers (Parameter 'Number of Rx Segments') ist used to generate the amount of RX descriptors and normal RX buffers. Due to the delayed RX buffer release for the Zero Copy feature RX descriptors could be blocked and the paket reception would stop. During a delayed buffer release the extended buffers are used to assign them to the RX descriptor, while a buffer release is still pending.
The packet number that can be received during a burst may be limited to 'Number of Rx Segments', but with the additional buffers it is still possible receive this burst while buffer releases are still pending.

Restrictions: The amount of normal and additional buffers must not exceed a total of 255.

########## 3. EthRxSegmentSize

- **UUID**: `bc47ae79-1fff-4478-9a81-e62e4558b25a`
- **参数名**: `EthRxSegmentSize`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `1522`
- **取值范围**: [60..1600]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines the size of a segment the receive buffer is built of.

Multiple of these segments are used to hold a complete Ethernet frame.

########## 4. EthRxMaxFrameSize

- **UUID**: `11370d7d-a2f8-4ddd-a941-5741e7604b6f`
- **参数名**: `EthRxMaxFrameSize`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `1522`
- **取值范围**: [60..1600]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines the maximum length of an Ethernet frame, which is allowed to be received.

####### 3. EthTxBufConfig

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `09f5a388-3a22-4095-84fd-0bfa8d8ce621`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `EthTxBufConfig`
- **多重性**: [1..infinite]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

This container includes the configuration of a Tx buffer.

######## 参数配置

此容器包含 **2** 个参数：

######### INTEGER 类型参数

########## 1. EthCtrlTxBufLenByte

- **UUID**: `93403e0d-d8bb-4016-83e6-96edb188a005`
- **参数名**: `EthCtrlTxBufLenByte`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `1522`
- **取值范围**: [64..1522]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value limits the maximum length (including Ethernet header + payload + CRC) of the Ethernet frame in bytes.
In case VLAN is enabled the Ethernet header length is 18, otherwise 14. The maximum payload length is 1500 bytes and the minimum length is 46 bytes. The CRC consists of 4 bytes.

Example:
- Configuring a buffer that shall hold a VLAN tagged Ethernet frame with a maximum payload of 1500 bytes, the overall frame length is 1500 bytes payload + 18 bytes header + 4 bytes CRC = 1522 bytes.
- Configuring a buffer that shall hold an Ethernet frame without VLAN tag and a minimum payload of 46 bytes, the overall frame length is 46 bytes payload + 14 bytes header + 4 bytes CRC = 64 bytes.

########## 2. EthTxBufTotal

- **UUID**: `36542667-1448-49fb-9b51-74689cd65603`
- **参数名**: `EthTxBufTotal`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `2`
- **取值范围**: [2..255]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value specifies the number of transmit buffers.

- **参数名**: `EthDeviceIdentifier`

########## 3. EthResManagerPassword

- **UUID**: `d6ed4419-ff5a-4b34-bc02-348bbbaae518`
- **参数名**: `EthResManagerPassword`
- **数据类型**: LINKER-SYMBOL
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **字符串变体**:
  - 默认值: `NULL`
  - 最大长度: 254
  - 最小长度: 1
- **描述**: This paramter defines the password used to grant access to the Resource Manager of INTEGRITY.

This password is used within calls to the INTEGRITY Resource Manager (e.g. RequestResource()) to retrieve the needed OS objects for IO Device, register memory region and so on.

If no password is used by the Resource Manager NULL must be provided as value to signal the omit of the password check.

########## 4. EthRegMemRegionPool

  - 最小长度: 1
- **描述**: This parameter defines the Memory Region Pool where the virtual Memory Region to map the registers into the virtual address space is drawn from.

- **容器名称**: `EthInterruptServiceRoutine`
- **多重性**: [0..infinite]

This container defines an ISR which aggregates the possible events that result in issuing an interrupt.

########## 参数配置

此容器包含 **2** 个参数：

########### FUNCTION-NAME 类型参数

############ 1. EthPreIsrUserFunction

- **UUID**: `a76a7deb-fab5-4622-8768-c225c79b2964`
- **参数名**: `EthPreIsrUserFunction`
- **数据类型**: FUNCTION-NAME
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **函数变体**:
  - 默认值: `NULL_PTR`
- **描述**: This parameter defines a user function that shall be called at the start of the ISR.

The function declaration must be provided by a header file added to EthAdditionalIncludes in the EthGeneral configuration container.

Function signature shall be as followed:
void <UserFunction>(uint8 CtrlIdx);

NULL_PTR: No user function shall be called.

############ 2. EthPostIsrUserFunction

- **UUID**: `6490f07d-48b1-476a-8600-7ace8c0f99a2`

- **需要索引**: false
- **函数变体**:
  - 默认值: `NULL_PTR`
- **描述**: This parameter defines a user function that shall be called at the end of the ISR.

The function declaration must be provided by a header file added to EthAdditionalIncludes in the EthGeneral configuration container.

Function signature shall be as followed:
void <UserFunction>(uint8 CtrlIdx);

NULL_PTR: No user function shall be called.

- **容器名称**: `EthInterruptServiceRoutine`
- **多重性**: [0..infinite]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

This container defines an ISR which aggregates the possible events that result in issuing an interrupt.

######## 参数配置

此容器包含 **2** 个参数：

######### FUNCTION-NAME 类型参数

########## 1. EthPreIsrUserFunction

- **UUID**: `a76a7deb-fab5-4622-8768-c225c79b2964`
- **参数名**: `EthPreIsrUserFunction`
- **数据类型**: FUNCTION-NAME
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **函数变体**:
  - 默认值: `NULL_PTR`
- **描述**: This parameter defines a user function that shall be called at the start of the ISR.

The function declaration must be provided by a header file added to EthAdditionalIncludes in the EthGeneral configuration container.

Function signature shall be as followed:
void <UserFunction>(uint8 CtrlIdx);

NULL_PTR: No user function shall be called.

########## 2. EthPostIsrUserFunction

- **UUID**: `6490f07d-48b1-476a-8600-7ace8c0f99a2`
- **参数名**: `EthPostIsrUserFunction`
- **数据类型**: FUNCTION-NAME
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **函数变体**:
  - 默认值: `NULL_PTR`
- **描述**: This parameter defines a user function that shall be called at the end of the ISR.

The function declaration must be provided by a header file added to EthAdditionalIncludes in the EthGeneral configuration container.

Function signature shall be as followed:
void <UserFunction>(uint8 CtrlIdx);

NULL_PTR: No user function shall be called.

- **UUID**: `554c3bed-768d-4ac3-933f-7cbbe3cefa27`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `EthZeroCopyConfig`
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

This container contains the configuration for Zero-Copy related parameters.

######## 参数配置

此容器包含 **3** 个参数：

######### INTEGER 类型参数

########## 1. EthCtrlMaxExtBufNum

- **UUID**: `14f72291-2e68-4265-a299-e48697a75c78`
- **参数名**: `EthCtrlMaxExtBufNum`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `2`
- **取值范围**: [1..255]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value specifies the maximum number of external transmission buffers that can be injected simultaniously. These buffers are provided by the user and the driver only keeps track of their state when used.

######### FUNCTION-NAME 类型参数

########## 1. EthCtrlExtTxBufConfirmationCallout

- **UUID**: `c4a90548-e8d0-4a35-8959-aa2f9177df7a`
- **参数名**: `EthCtrlExtTxBufConfirmationCallout`
- **数据类型**: FUNCTION-NAME
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **函数变体**:
  - 默认值: `NULL_PTR`
- **描述**: This parameter defines the alternative function to be called upon the confirmation of an Ethernet frame based on external transmission buffers.
If the EthIf is not present in the SIP, then this parameter is mandatory, else optional.

Means: If the EthIf is present in the SIP and this callout is given, then all external buffer TX confirmations are calling the EthCtrlExtTxBufConfirmationCallout.

The signature of the function specified in this parameter has to match the signature of the EthIf_TxConfirmation used in the delivery.

########## 2. EthAddRxSegmentsDepletedCallout

- **UUID**: `54a3c642-d170-4f37-902d-f23901eb100f`
- **参数名**: `EthAddRxSegmentsDepletedCallout`
- **数据类型**: FUNCTION-NAME
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **函数变体**:
  - 默认值: `NULL_PTR`
- **描述**: This parameter defines a function, which will be called, if all additional Zero Copy Rx Buffers of a queue are depleted and received frames are being discarded, because the user application does not release the Rx Buffers.

The callout is declared as pointer to function
void AddRxSegmentsDepletedCallout(uint8 ctrlIdx, uint8 rxQueueId)

####### 19. EthQueueManagement

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `c88589c4-31a4-4686-ab75-1e13f0030eee`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `EthQueueManagement`
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

This container includes the configuration rx queue configuration. Rx queues in this container are used to map different receivers. (other than standard Ethif Module)
This container includes the configuration which is necessary for extended Rx queue managment.
Based on the rx queue filtering, rx indication of the respective queue is provided to a external module.

######## 引用关系

此容器包含 **1** 个引用：

######### REFERENCE 类型引用

########## 1. EthQueue0RxBufConfigRef

- **UUID**: `94d9245b-b40a-4be4-b8e0-89749318cc67`
- **引用名**: `EthQueue0RxBufConfigRef`
- **引用类型**: REFERENCE
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **目标**: `EthRxBufConfig`
- **完整路径**: `/MICROSAR/Eth_30_Tc3xx/Eth/EthConfigSet/EthCtrlConfig/EthRxBufConfig`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Reference to the default queue, where all received frames, which do not match configured reception filter of any other queue, will be placed.

######## 配置容器

本模块包含 **1** 个配置容器：

######### 1. EthExtendedRxQueue

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `886271e8-4315-414c-9afc-9daa50c80cbe`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `EthExtendedRxQueue`
- **多重性**: [1..infinite]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

This container includes the configuration for the Reception Queue Management. If several of the matching parameters in this container are configured, all criteria must be satisfied for the frame to be mapped to the respective queue.

########## 参数配置

此容器包含 **2** 个参数：

########### INTEGER 类型参数

############ 1. EthQueueIndex

- **UUID**: `648e4dab-3eb7-4048-bdfa-80f4979dde2b`
- **参数名**: `EthQueueIndex`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..255]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines the index of the queue. This is set by DaVinci Configurator automatically.

########### FUNCTION-NAME 类型参数

############ 1. EthExtendedRxIndicationCallout

- **UUID**: `f55f52a4-2290-4948-97a6-0ff59b22dd94`
- **参数名**: `EthExtendedRxIndicationCallout`
- **数据类型**: FUNCTION-NAME
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **函数变体**:
  - 默认值: `NULL_PTR`
- **描述**: This parameter defines the queue specific alternative function to be called upon the reception of an Ethernet frame instead of the EthIf_RxIndication.
Deleting this parameter disables the functionality and the EthIf_RxIndication is called again.

The callout is declared as pointer to function
void ExtendedRxIndicationCallout(uint8 ctrlIdx, uint8 rxBufIdx, Eth_FrameType frameType, uint8* rxBufStartPtr, uint16 offsetToHeaderStart, uint16 offsetToPayloadStart, uint16 payloadLength)

/MICROSAR/Eth/EthGeneral/EthAdditionalIncludes parameter is used to add the header file for this callout.

- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Reference to the EthRxBufferConfig which is used by this queue.

####### 21. EthMemoryRemapping

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `ecbc1ac0-7b13-4d16-8b1c-9909a3dfa0be`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `EthMemoryRemapping`
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **需要索引**: false

######## 参数配置

此容器包含 **7** 个参数：

######### INTEGER 类型参数

########## 1. EthCtrlMemoryRemappingOffset

- **UUID**: `32a781a7-4e49-4dad-8f9a-82d660dbd630`
- **参数名**: `EthCtrlMemoryRemappingOffset`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: The offset between local memory address (CPU address) and global memory address (SoC address). This parameter needs to be set when the memory remapping is enabled.

########## 2. EthCtrlLocalAddressLowerBorder

- **UUID**: `7257380a-f382-4ff1-8700-eb64997c3746`
- **参数名**: `EthCtrlLocalAddressLowerBorder`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Lower border for local memory address (CPU address). This parameter needs to be set when the memory remapping is enabled.

########## 3. EthCtrlLocalAddressUpperBorder

- **UUID**: `2af9f3ca-7d67-44eb-9a3b-b11e7565053e`
- **参数名**: `EthCtrlLocalAddressUpperBorder`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Upper border for local memory address (CPU address). This parameter needs to be set when the memory remapping is enabled. This address is the first address value excluded from the local memory address range.

########## 4. EthCtrlGlobalAddressLowerBorder

- **UUID**: `7bcd05c6-0e61-4ad8-9bac-66557f86e34f`
- **参数名**: `EthCtrlGlobalAddressLowerBorder`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Lower border for global memory address (SoC address). This parameter needs to be set when the memory remapping is enabled.

########## 5. EthCtrlGlobalAddressUpperBorder

- **UUID**: `9d99033f-8552-47d6-8e23-ae07ce1d558c`
- **参数名**: `EthCtrlGlobalAddressUpperBorder`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Upper border for global memory address (SoC address). This parameter needs to be set when the memory remapping is enabled. This address is the first address value excluded from the global memory address range.

######### BOOLEAN 类型参数

########## 1. EthCtrlEnableMemoryRemapping

- **UUID**: `24efd349-619a-445f-999d-c12b00e1883b`
- **参数名**: `EthCtrlEnableMemoryRemapping`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines if the memory remapping is enabled. This parameter should be enabled when the memory region (which is used for Ethernet descriptors/buffers) have different CPU address (the address in the view of CPU) and SoC address (the address in the view of the peripheries, e. g. the DMA).

########## 2. EthCtrlMemoryRemappingNegativeOffset

- **UUID**: `708fbfe6-5120-4e59-9330-2fdc6398f9f2`
- **参数名**: `EthCtrlMemoryRemappingNegativeOffset`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines if the memory remapping offset is negative. Therefore, the local address borders shall have a greater value than the global address borders.

Restrictions: 'EthCtrlEnableRxInterrupt' must be equal for all controllers and equal to 'EthEnableRxInterrupt'

- **参数名**: `EthCtrlEnableTxInterrupt`

######## 4. EthCtrlEnableMacWriteAccess

- **UUID**: `9f652936-bfd5-483e-9370-c66cd3008ce9`
- **参数名**: `EthCtrlEnableMacWriteAccess`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines if access to an NvM block containing the MAC address and the corresponding API is enabled.

######## 5. EthCtrlEnableQoS

- **UUID**: `9f30d178-2514-4049-8614-46d26a7a5996`
- **参数名**: `EthCtrlEnableQoS`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines if QoS (Quality of Service) is enabled.

VLAN tagged frames are prioritized according to their VLAN priority. The priority handling is taken into account during reception and transmission of frames.

######## 6. EthCtrlEnablePtp

- **UUID**: `15e67c24-dae9-4452-91b4-e6ad3c47ffd8`
- **参数名**: `EthCtrlEnablePtp`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines if Precision-Time-Protocol support is enabled or not. PTP is mostly used in combination with Audio Video Bridging (AVB)

######## 7. EthEnableAVBConformance

- **UUID**: `e2ae304a-7839-4bee-9471-2bb9c2e3d622`
- **参数名**: `EthEnableAVBConformance`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines if the QoS/FQTSS features a used for a AVB or generalized usecase.
In the AVB usecase a maximum  of 75% of the Port Transmit Rate can be used for traffic class A and B. Only frames with a frametype related to AVB are passed to the queues for traffic class A and B.

######## 8. EthCtrlEnableVlanIDBasedFramesQueuing

- **UUID**: `b1f4f946-6d5d-4808-9a15-b8454e750859`
- **参数名**: `EthCtrlEnableVlanIDBasedFramesQueuing`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines if the Ethernet frames are queued based on VLAN ID
VLAN tagged frames are assigned to the TX/RX queues according to their VLAN ID. The VLAN-ID and the queue mapping shall be configured by the user.

######## 9. EthCtrlEnableQueueManagement

- **UUID**: `33973e7a-bdc0-4450-8460-b4d1fae69fce`
- **参数名**: `EthCtrlEnableQueueManagement`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false

######## 10. EthCtrlEnableOsfMode

- **UUID**: `d2625ab7-0979-4032-a9f3-2b1eba41316f`
- **参数名**: `EthCtrlEnableOsfMode`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `true`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines whether OSF mode is used for frame transmission. When OSF mode is enabled, the processing of a second Tx-descriptor is started before the first one is transmitted completely. This results in increased throughput in transmission.

######## 11. EthCtrlEnablePpsEventOnTimeApi

- **UUID**: `a16e49c7-872d-4953-b33a-16f1bece9026`
- **参数名**: `EthCtrlEnablePpsEventOnTimeApi`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter enables the ArmPpsEventOnTime Api. The Api is used for PPS sub-module of the Ethernet MAC to trigger an event on the PPS line at the given globalTime.

####### STRING 类型参数

######## 1. EthCtrlPhysAddr

- **UUID**: `424ff68f-f4b1-4015-a92a-46689984c1dd`
- **参数名**: `EthCtrlPhysAddr`
- **数据类型**: STRING
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **字符串变体**:
  - 默认值: `00:00:00:00:00:01`
- **描述**: This parameter defines the Controller Physical Hardware Address (MAC-Address). The input format has to match xx:xx:xx:xx:xx:xx, where x stands for a hex value between 0 and F.

####### ENUMERATION 类型参数

######## 1. EthCtrlEnableTrafficShaping

- **UUID**: `05caa505-e596-4ffa-8383-9deb0c413813`
- **参数名**: `EthCtrlEnableTrafficShaping`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `NO_TRAFFIC_SHAPING`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `NO_TRAFFIC_SHAPING` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `STATIC_TRAFFIC_SHAPING` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DYNAMIC_TRAFFIC_SHAPING` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **描述**: This parameter defines whether Traffic Shaping is used. Therefore the ethernet controller uses an algorithm like CBS (Credit Based Shaping) for limiting the maximum bandwidth per queue.

######## 2. EthCtrlMiiInterface

- **UUID**: `5d6ed626-12fe-4a40-b907-e1ffc80428a3`
- **参数名**: `EthCtrlMiiInterface`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `ETH_MII_MODE`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `ETH_MII_MODE` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `ETH_RMII_MODE` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `ETH_RGMII_MODE` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **描述**: This parameter defines the interface type used to connect PHY and MAC.

Please refere to the derivative's reference manual and the board schematic to retrieve the correct management interface type.

######## 3. EthCtrlCsrClkRange

- **UUID**: `094773ef-458f-4929-b4d6-78f570a68393`
- **参数名**: `EthCtrlCsrClkRange`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `CLK_20_35_MHZ`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `CLK_20_35_MHZ` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `CLK_35_60_MHZ` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `CLK_60_100_MHZ` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `CLK_100_150_MHZ` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `CLK_150_250_MHZ` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `CLK_250_300_MHZ` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `CLK_300_500_MHZ` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `CLK_500_800_MHZ` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **描述**: The CSR clock range determines the frequency of the MDC clock according to CSR clock frequnecy.

The MDC clock should be approximately between 1.0 MHz to 2.5 MHz.

######## 4. EthCtrlPrtSpdSel

- **UUID**: `1538963a-34e8-4d9e-8f50-8d24d460811c`
- **参数名**: `EthCtrlPrtSpdSel`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]

  - `PS_10_MBITS` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `PS_100_MBITS` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `PS_1000_MBITS` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **描述**: This parameter selects the ethernet port speed (10Mbit/s, 100Mbit/s and 1000Mbit/s) .

####### FUNCTION-NAME 类型参数

######## 1. EthCtrlPeripheralRegionOsIdentifier

- **UUID**: `d705db2e-33ff-49bd-ae62-c731f11aca19`
- **参数名**: `EthCtrlPeripheralRegionOsIdentifier`
- **数据类型**: FUNCTION-NAME
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Unique C Identifier which is used by the Ethernet Controller driver for accessing the OS configured peripheral region.

######## 2. EthRxIndicationCallout

- **UUID**: `5247fdab-0071-4d60-8f80-7e3fafb93a32`
- **参数名**: `EthRxIndicationCallout`
- **数据类型**: FUNCTION-NAME
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **函数变体**:
  - 默认值: `NULL_PTR`
- **描述**: This parameter defines the alternative function to be called upon the reception of an Ethernet frame instead of the EthIf_RxIndication.

NULL_PTR as value disables the functionality and the EthIf_RxIndication is called again.

The signature of the function specified in this parameter has to match the signature of the EthIf_RxIndication used in the delivery.

######## 3. EthTxConfirmationCallout

- **UUID**: `90e35b55-8320-4c78-8617-6bfcf2c5a4a2`
- **参数名**: `EthTxConfirmationCallout`
- **数据类型**: FUNCTION-NAME
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **函数变体**:
  - 默认值: `NULL_PTR`
- **描述**: This parameter defines the alternative function to be called upon the confirmation of an Ethernet frame instead of the EthIf_TxConfirmation.

NULL_PTR as value disables the functionality and the EthIf_TxConfirmation is called again.

The signature of the function specified in this parameter has to match the signature of the EthIf_TxConfirmation used in the delivery.

######## 4. EthRawTxConfirmationCallout

- **UUID**: `0425e5b0-c7f5-426b-ad66-365d5cac3220`
- **参数名**: `EthRawTxConfirmationCallout`
- **数据类型**: FUNCTION-NAME
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **函数变体**:
  - 默认值: `NULL_PTR`
- **描述**: This parameter defines the used Tx confirmation callout for EthEnableRawTxApi.
WARNING: EthIf_TxConfirmation() is never called for raw transmission requests!
The signature of the function specified in this parameter has to match the signature of the EthIf_TxConfirmation used in the delivery.

- **参数名**: `EthRxExtSegmentNumber`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `2`
- **取值范围**: [1..255]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value specifies the maximum expected number of extended buffers that be used for frame receiving.
The amount of normal buffers (Parameter 'Number of Rx Segments') ist used to generate the amount of RX descriptors and normal RX buffers. Due to the delayed RX buffer release for the Zero Copy feature RX descriptors could be blocked and the paket reception would stop. During a delayed buffer release the extended buffers are used to assign them to the RX descriptor, while a buffer release is still pending.
The packet number that can be received during a burst may be limited to 'Number of Rx Segments', but with the additional buffers it is still possible receive this burst while buffer releases are still pending.

Restrictions: The amount of normal and additional buffers must not exceed a total of 255.

########## 3. EthRxSegmentSize

- **UUID**: `bc47ae79-1fff-4478-9a81-e62e4558b25a`
- **参数名**: `EthRxSegmentSize`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `1522`
- **取值范围**: [60..1600]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines the size of a segment the receive buffer is built of.

Multiple of these segments are used to hold a complete Ethernet frame.

########## 4. EthRxMaxFrameSize

- **UUID**: `11370d7d-a2f8-4ddd-a941-5741e7604b6f`
- **参数名**: `EthRxMaxFrameSize`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `1522`
- **取值范围**: [60..1600]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines the maximum length of an Ethernet frame, which is allowed to be received.

####### 2. EthTxBufConfig

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `09f5a388-3a22-4095-84fd-0bfa8d8ce621`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `EthTxBufConfig`
- **多重性**: [1..infinite]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

This container includes the configuration of a Tx buffer.

######## 参数配置

此容器包含 **2** 个参数：

######### INTEGER 类型参数

########## 1. EthCtrlTxBufLenByte

- **UUID**: `93403e0d-d8bb-4016-83e6-96edb188a005`
- **参数名**: `EthCtrlTxBufLenByte`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `1522`
- **取值范围**: [64..1522]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value limits the maximum length (including Ethernet header + payload + CRC) of the Ethernet frame in bytes.
In case VLAN is enabled the Ethernet header length is 18, otherwise 14. The maximum payload length is 1500 bytes and the minimum length is 46 bytes. The CRC consists of 4 bytes.

Example:
- Configuring a buffer that shall hold a VLAN tagged Ethernet frame with a maximum payload of 1500 bytes, the overall frame length is 1500 bytes payload + 18 bytes header + 4 bytes CRC = 1522 bytes.
- Configuring a buffer that shall hold an Ethernet frame without VLAN tag and a minimum payload of 46 bytes, the overall frame length is 46 bytes payload + 14 bytes header + 4 bytes CRC = 64 bytes.

########## 2. EthTxBufTotal

- **UUID**: `36542667-1448-49fb-9b51-74689cd65603`
- **参数名**: `EthTxBufTotal`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `2`
- **取值范围**: [2..255]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value specifies the number of transmit buffers.

########## 1. EthRegMemRegionIdentifier

########## 2. EthDeviceIdentifier

- **UUID**: `115abc0e-5fbd-4957-8f09-7d12c761786a`
- **参数名**: `EthDeviceIdentifier`
- **数据类型**: LINKER-SYMBOL
- **多重性**: [1..1]

  - 最大长度: 254
  - 最小长度: 1
- **描述**: This parameter defines the identifier used to identify the IO Device object representing the Ethernet controller within INTEGRITY.

########## 3. EthResManagerPassword

- **UUID**: `d6ed4419-ff5a-4b34-bc02-348bbbaae518`
- **参数名**: `EthResManagerPassword`
- **数据类型**: LINKER-SYMBOL
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **字符串变体**:
  - 默认值: `NULL`
  - 最大长度: 254
  - 最小长度: 1
- **描述**: This paramter defines the password used to grant access to the Resource Manager of INTEGRITY.

This password is used within calls to the INTEGRITY Resource Manager (e.g. RequestResource()) to retrieve the needed OS objects for IO Device, register memory region and so on.

If no password is used by the Resource Manager NULL must be provided as value to signal the omit of the password check.

########## 4. EthRegMemRegionPool

- **字符串变体**:
  - 默认值: `__ghs_VirtualMemoryRegionPool`
  - 最小长度: 1
- **描述**: This parameter defines the Memory Region Pool where the virtual Memory Region to map the registers into the virtual address space is drawn from.

########## 5. EthAsrOsWrapperIsrNo

- **需要索引**: false

NULL_PTR: No user function shall be called.

####### 17. EthZeroCopyConfig

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `554c3bed-768d-4ac3-933f-7cbbe3cefa27`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `EthZeroCopyConfig`
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

This container contains the configuration for Zero-Copy related parameters.

######## 参数配置

此容器包含 **3** 个参数：

######### INTEGER 类型参数

########## 1. EthCtrlMaxExtBufNum

- **UUID**: `14f72291-2e68-4265-a299-e48697a75c78`
- **参数名**: `EthCtrlMaxExtBufNum`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `2`
- **取值范围**: [1..255]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value specifies the maximum number of external transmission buffers that can be injected simultaniously. These buffers are provided by the user and the driver only keeps track of their state when used.

######### FUNCTION-NAME 类型参数

########## 1. EthCtrlExtTxBufConfirmationCallout

- **UUID**: `c4a90548-e8d0-4a35-8959-aa2f9177df7a`
- **参数名**: `EthCtrlExtTxBufConfirmationCallout`
- **数据类型**: FUNCTION-NAME
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **函数变体**:
  - 默认值: `NULL_PTR`
- **描述**: This parameter defines the alternative function to be called upon the confirmation of an Ethernet frame based on external transmission buffers.
If the EthIf is not present in the SIP, then this parameter is mandatory, else optional.

Means: If the EthIf is present in the SIP and this callout is given, then all external buffer TX confirmations are calling the EthCtrlExtTxBufConfirmationCallout.

The signature of the function specified in this parameter has to match the signature of the EthIf_TxConfirmation used in the delivery.

########## 2. EthAddRxSegmentsDepletedCallout

- **UUID**: `54a3c642-d170-4f37-902d-f23901eb100f`
- **参数名**: `EthAddRxSegmentsDepletedCallout`
- **数据类型**: FUNCTION-NAME
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **函数变体**:
  - 默认值: `NULL_PTR`
- **描述**: This parameter defines a function, which will be called, if all additional Zero Copy Rx Buffers of a queue are depleted and received frames are being discarded, because the user application does not release the Rx Buffers.

The callout is declared as pointer to function
void AddRxSegmentsDepletedCallout(uint8 ctrlIdx, uint8 rxQueueId)

####### 18. EthQueueManagement

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `c88589c4-31a4-4686-ab75-1e13f0030eee`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `EthQueueManagement`
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

This container includes the configuration rx queue configuration. Rx queues in this container are used to map different receivers. (other than standard Ethif Module)
This container includes the configuration which is necessary for extended Rx queue managment.
Based on the rx queue filtering, rx indication of the respective queue is provided to a external module.

######## 引用关系

此容器包含 **1** 个引用：

######### REFERENCE 类型引用

- **目标**: `EthRxBufConfig`
- **完整路径**: `/MICROSAR/Eth_30_Tc3xx/Eth/EthConfigSet/EthCtrlConfig/EthRxBufConfig`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Reference to the default queue, where all received frames, which do not match configured reception filter of any other queue, will be placed.

######## 配置容器

本模块包含 **1** 个配置容器：

######### 1. EthExtendedRxQueue

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `886271e8-4315-414c-9afc-9daa50c80cbe`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `EthExtendedRxQueue`
- **多重性**: [1..infinite]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

This container includes the configuration for the Reception Queue Management. If several of the matching parameters in this container are configured, all criteria must be satisfied for the frame to be mapped to the respective queue.

########## 参数配置

此容器包含 **2** 个参数：

########### INTEGER 类型参数

############ 1. EthQueueIndex

- **UUID**: `648e4dab-3eb7-4048-bdfa-80f4979dde2b`
- **参数名**: `EthQueueIndex`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..255]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines the index of the queue. This is set by DaVinci Configurator automatically.

########### FUNCTION-NAME 类型参数

############ 1. EthExtendedRxIndicationCallout

- **UUID**: `f55f52a4-2290-4948-97a6-0ff59b22dd94`
- **参数名**: `EthExtendedRxIndicationCallout`
- **数据类型**: FUNCTION-NAME
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **函数变体**:
  - 默认值: `NULL_PTR`
- **描述**: This parameter defines the queue specific alternative function to be called upon the reception of an Ethernet frame instead of the EthIf_RxIndication.
Deleting this parameter disables the functionality and the EthIf_RxIndication is called again.

The callout is declared as pointer to function
void ExtendedRxIndicationCallout(uint8 ctrlIdx, uint8 rxBufIdx, Eth_FrameType frameType, uint8* rxBufStartPtr, uint16 offsetToHeaderStart, uint16 offsetToPayloadStart, uint16 payloadLength)

/MICROSAR/Eth/EthGeneral/EthAdditionalIncludes parameter is used to add the header file for this callout.

########## 1. EthCtrlMemoryRemappingOffset

- **参数名**: `EthCtrlMemoryRemappingOffset`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: The offset between local memory address (CPU address) and global memory address (SoC address). This parameter needs to be set when the memory remapping is enabled.

########## 2. EthCtrlLocalAddressLowerBorder

- **UUID**: `7257380a-f382-4ff1-8700-eb64997c3746`
- **参数名**: `EthCtrlLocalAddressLowerBorder`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Lower border for local memory address (CPU address). This parameter needs to be set when the memory remapping is enabled.

########## 3. EthCtrlLocalAddressUpperBorder

- **UUID**: `2af9f3ca-7d67-44eb-9a3b-b11e7565053e`
- **参数名**: `EthCtrlLocalAddressUpperBorder`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Upper border for local memory address (CPU address). This parameter needs to be set when the memory remapping is enabled. This address is the first address value excluded from the local memory address range.

########## 4. EthCtrlGlobalAddressLowerBorder

- **UUID**: `7bcd05c6-0e61-4ad8-9bac-66557f86e34f`
- **参数名**: `EthCtrlGlobalAddressLowerBorder`

########## 5. EthCtrlGlobalAddressUpperBorder

######### BOOLEAN 类型参数

########## 1. EthCtrlEnableMemoryRemapping

- **UUID**: `24efd349-619a-445f-999d-c12b00e1883b`
- **参数名**: `EthCtrlEnableMemoryRemapping`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines if the memory remapping is enabled. This parameter should be enabled when the memory region (which is used for Ethernet descriptors/buffers) have different CPU address (the address in the view of CPU) and SoC address (the address in the view of the peripheries, e. g. the DMA).

########## 2. EthCtrlMemoryRemappingNegativeOffset

- **UUID**: `708fbfe6-5120-4e59-9330-2fdc6398f9f2`
- **参数名**: `EthCtrlMemoryRemappingNegativeOffset`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines if the memory remapping offset is negative. Therefore, the local address borders shall have a greater value than the global address borders.
