##### 4. EthRxBufConfig

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `d22e3856-672b-49ca-a5e2-a15464127dd5`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `EthRxBufConfig`
- **多重性**: [1..infinite]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

This container includes the configuration of an Rx buffer.

###### 参数配置

此容器包含 **4** 个参数：

####### INTEGER 类型参数

######## 1. EthRxSegmentNumber

- **UUID**: `f1b87058-cd85-4537-a817-8463907870c4`
- **参数名**: `EthRxSegmentNumber`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `2`
- **取值范围**: [2..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value defines the number of Rx segments the receiver buffer is assembled of.

######## 2. EthRxExtSegmentNumber

- **UUID**: `2185694c-4c71-49ad-a1c9-f1692eeea1b4`
- **参数名**: `EthRxExtSegmentNumber`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `2`
- **取值范围**: [1..255]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value specifies the maximum expected number of extended buffers that be used for frame receiving.
The amount of normal buffers (Parameter 'Number of Rx Segments') ist used to generate the amount of RX descriptors and normal RX buffers. Due to the delayed RX buffer release for the Zero Copy feature RX descriptors could be blocked and the paket reception would stop. During a delayed buffer release the extended buffers are used to assign them to the RX descriptor, while a buffer release is still pending.
The packet number that can be received during a burst may be limited to 'Number of Rx Segments', but with the additional buffers it is still possible receive this burst while buffer releases are still pending.

Restrictions: The amount of normal and additional buffers must not exceed a total of 255.

######## 3. EthRxSegmentSize

- **UUID**: `bc47ae79-1fff-4478-9a81-e62e4558b25a`
- **参数名**: `EthRxSegmentSize`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `1522`
- **取值范围**: [60..1600]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines the size of a segment the receive buffer is built of.

Multiple of these segments are used to hold a complete Ethernet frame.

######## 4. EthRxMaxFrameSize

- **UUID**: `11370d7d-a2f8-4ddd-a941-5741e7604b6f`
- **参数名**: `EthRxMaxFrameSize`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `1522`
- **取值范围**: [60..1600]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines the maximum length of an Ethernet frame, which is allowed to be received.

##### 5. EthTxBufConfig

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `09f5a388-3a22-4095-84fd-0bfa8d8ce621`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `EthTxBufConfig`
- **多重性**: [1..infinite]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

This container includes the configuration of a Tx buffer.

###### 参数配置

此容器包含 **2** 个参数：

####### INTEGER 类型参数

######## 1. EthCtrlTxBufLenByte

- **UUID**: `93403e0d-d8bb-4016-83e6-96edb188a005`
- **参数名**: `EthCtrlTxBufLenByte`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `1522`
- **取值范围**: [64..1522]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value limits the maximum length (including Ethernet header + payload + CRC) of the Ethernet frame in bytes.
In case VLAN is enabled the Ethernet header length is 18, otherwise 14. The maximum payload length is 1500 bytes and the minimum length is 46 bytes. The CRC consists of 4 bytes.

Example:
- Configuring a buffer that shall hold a VLAN tagged Ethernet frame with a maximum payload of 1500 bytes, the overall frame length is 1500 bytes payload + 18 bytes header + 4 bytes CRC = 1522 bytes.
- Configuring a buffer that shall hold an Ethernet frame without VLAN tag and a minimum payload of 46 bytes, the overall frame length is 46 bytes payload + 14 bytes header + 4 bytes CRC = 64 bytes.

######## 2. EthTxBufTotal

- **UUID**: `36542667-1448-49fb-9b51-74689cd65603`
- **参数名**: `EthTxBufTotal`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `2`
- **取值范围**: [2..255]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This value specifies the number of transmit buffers.

######## 3. EthResManagerPassword

- **UUID**: `d6ed4419-ff5a-4b34-bc02-348bbbaae518`
- **参数名**: `EthResManagerPassword`
- **数据类型**: LINKER-SYMBOL
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **字符串变体**:
  - 默认值: `NULL`
  - 最大长度: 254
  - 最小长度: 1
- **描述**: This paramter defines the password used to grant access to the Resource Manager of INTEGRITY.

This password is used within calls to the INTEGRITY Resource Manager (e.g. RequestResource()) to retrieve the needed OS objects for IO Device, register memory region and so on.

If no password is used by the Resource Manager NULL must be provided as value to signal the omit of the password check.

- **描述**: This parameter defines the Memory Region Pool where the virtual Memory Region to map the registers into the virtual address space is drawn from.

######## 参数配置

此容器包含 **2** 个参数：

######### FUNCTION-NAME 类型参数

########## 1. EthPreIsrUserFunction

- **UUID**: `a76a7deb-fab5-4622-8768-c225c79b2964`
- **参数名**: `EthPreIsrUserFunction`
- **数据类型**: FUNCTION-NAME
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **函数变体**:
  - 默认值: `NULL_PTR`
- **描述**: This parameter defines a user function that shall be called at the start of the ISR.

The function declaration must be provided by a header file added to EthAdditionalIncludes in the EthGeneral configuration container.

Function signature shall be as followed:
void <UserFunction>(uint8 CtrlIdx);

NULL_PTR: No user function shall be called.

########## 2. EthPostIsrUserFunction

- **UUID**: `6490f07d-48b1-476a-8600-7ace8c0f99a2`

- **需要索引**: false
- **函数变体**:
  - 默认值: `NULL_PTR`
- **描述**: This parameter defines a user function that shall be called at the end of the ISR.

The function declaration must be provided by a header file added to EthAdditionalIncludes in the EthGeneral configuration container.

Function signature shall be as followed:
void <UserFunction>(uint8 CtrlIdx);

NULL_PTR: No user function shall be called.

This container defines an ISR which aggregates the possible events that result in issuing an interrupt.

###### 参数配置

此容器包含 **2** 个参数：

####### FUNCTION-NAME 类型参数

######## 1. EthPreIsrUserFunction

- **UUID**: `a76a7deb-fab5-4622-8768-c225c79b2964`
- **参数名**: `EthPreIsrUserFunction`
- **数据类型**: FUNCTION-NAME
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **函数变体**:
  - 默认值: `NULL_PTR`
- **描述**: This parameter defines a user function that shall be called at the start of the ISR.

The function declaration must be provided by a header file added to EthAdditionalIncludes in the EthGeneral configuration container.

Function signature shall be as followed:
void <UserFunction>(uint8 CtrlIdx);

NULL_PTR: No user function shall be called.

######## 2. EthPostIsrUserFunction

- **UUID**: `6490f07d-48b1-476a-8600-7ace8c0f99a2`

- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **函数变体**:
  - 默认值: `NULL_PTR`
- **描述**: This parameter defines a user function that shall be called at the end of the ISR.

The function declaration must be provided by a header file added to EthAdditionalIncludes in the EthGeneral configuration container.

Function signature shall be as followed:
void <UserFunction>(uint8 CtrlIdx);

NULL_PTR: No user function shall be called.

- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **函数变体**:
  - 默认值: `NULL_PTR`
- **描述**: This parameter defines the alternative function to be called upon the confirmation of an Ethernet frame based on external transmission buffers.
If the EthIf is not present in the SIP, then this parameter is mandatory, else optional.

Means: If the EthIf is present in the SIP and this callout is given, then all external buffer TX confirmations are calling the EthCtrlExtTxBufConfirmationCallout.

The signature of the function specified in this parameter has to match the signature of the EthIf_TxConfirmation used in the delivery.

######## 2. EthAddRxSegmentsDepletedCallout

- **UUID**: `54a3c642-d170-4f37-902d-f23901eb100f`
- **参数名**: `EthAddRxSegmentsDepletedCallout`
- **数据类型**: FUNCTION-NAME
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **函数变体**:
  - 默认值: `NULL_PTR`
- **描述**: This parameter defines a function, which will be called, if all additional Zero Copy Rx Buffers of a queue are depleted and received frames are being discarded, because the user application does not release the Rx Buffers.

The callout is declared as pointer to function
void AddRxSegmentsDepletedCallout(uint8 ctrlIdx, uint8 rxQueueId)

##### 21. EthQueueManagement

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `c88589c4-31a4-4686-ab75-1e13f0030eee`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `EthQueueManagement`
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

This container includes the configuration rx queue configuration. Rx queues in this container are used to map different receivers. (other than standard Ethif Module)
This container includes the configuration which is necessary for extended Rx queue managment.
Based on the rx queue filtering, rx indication of the respective queue is provided to a external module.

###### 引用关系

此容器包含 **1** 个引用：

####### REFERENCE 类型引用

######## 1. EthQueue0RxBufConfigRef

- **UUID**: `94d9245b-b40a-4be4-b8e0-89749318cc67`

- **引用类型**: REFERENCE
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **目标**: `EthRxBufConfig`
- **完整路径**: `/MICROSAR/Eth_30_Tc3xx/Eth/EthConfigSet/EthCtrlConfig/EthRxBufConfig`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Reference to the default queue, where all received frames, which do not match configured reception filter of any other queue, will be placed.

###### 配置容器

本模块包含 **1** 个配置容器：

- **UUID**: `2af9f3ca-7d67-44eb-9a3b-b11e7565053e`
- **参数名**: `EthCtrlLocalAddressUpperBorder`

- **描述**: Upper border for local memory address (CPU address). This parameter needs to be set when the memory remapping is enabled. This address is the first address value excluded from the local memory address range.

- **参数名**: `EthCtrlGlobalAddressUpperBorder`

####### BOOLEAN 类型参数

######## 1. EthCtrlEnableMemoryRemapping

- **UUID**: `24efd349-619a-445f-999d-c12b00e1883b`
- **参数名**: `EthCtrlEnableMemoryRemapping`

- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines if the memory remapping is enabled. This parameter should be enabled when the memory region (which is used for Ethernet descriptors/buffers) have different CPU address (the address in the view of CPU) and SoC address (the address in the view of the peripheries, e. g. the DMA).

######## 2. EthCtrlMemoryRemappingNegativeOffset

- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines if the memory remapping offset is negative. Therefore, the local address borders shall have a greater value than the global address borders.
